            justify-content: center;
            pointer-events: auto;
            font-family: monospace;
        }
        
        button {
            background: #0a1a2a;
            color: #00ff9d;
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            padding: 6px 14px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-family: monospace;
            transition: all 0.3s;
        }
        
        button:hover {
            background: #00ff9d;
            color: #050510;
        }

        /* Gizmo стили */
        .gizmo-container {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 20px;
            right: 20px;
            width: 120px;
            height: 120px;
            background: rgba(5,5,20,0.9);
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            border-radius: 8px;
            z-index: 1000;
            backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
            cursor: pointer;
        }

        .gizmo-axis {
            position: absolute;
            font-size: 12px;
            font-weight: bold;
            font-family: monospace;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }

        .gizmo-axis:hover {
            transform: scale(1.2);
            filter: drop-shadow(0 0 5px #00ff9d);
        }

        .gizmo-x { color: #ff4444; }
        .gizmo-y { color: #00ff9d; }
        .gizmo-z { color: #3B82F6; }
        
        .status-badge {
            display: inline-block;
            padding: 2px 6px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            font-size: 10px;
            margin-left: 8px;
        }
        
        .fps-counter {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            right: 20px;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
            padding: 4px 8px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            font-size: 10px;
            color: #00ff9d;
            font-family: monospace;
            z-index: 1000;
        }
        
        .agent-tooltip {
            position: absolute;
            background: #0a1a2a;
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            border-radius: 6px;
            padding: 8px 12px;
            color: #b7c9e2;
            font-size: 11px;
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 2000;
            display: none;
            backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
            font-family: monospace;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div class="fps-counter" id="fps" style="position: absolute; top: 20px; right: 20px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7); padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 10px; color: #00ff9d; z-index: 1000;">FPS: --</div>
    <div id="control-panel" style="position: absolute; top: 20px; right: 20px; background: rgba(10,14,26,0.95); border: 1px solid #00ff9d; border-radius: 8px; backdrop-filter: blur(10px); z-index: 1000; font-family: monospace; font-size: 11px; color: #b7c9e2; min-width: 200px;">
        <div id="panelHeader" style="padding: 12px 15px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; color: #00ff9d; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; border-bottom: 1px solid #2a3a4a;">
            УПРАВЛЕНИЕ <span id="panelToggleIcon" style="transition: transform 0.3s;">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div id="panelContent" style="padding: 12px 15px; transition: max-height 0.3s ease; overflow: hidden; max-height: 500px;">
            <div style="margin-bottom: 8px; padding: 4px; background: #1a2a3a; border-radius: 4px; text-align: center;">
                FPS: <span id="fpsValue" style="color: #00ff9d; font-weight: bold;">--</span>
            </div>
            
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleParticles" checked> Восходящие частицы
            </label>
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleStars" checked> Фоновые звезды
            </label>
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleGrid" checked> Сетка
            </label>
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleLinks" checked> Связи
            </label>
            
            <div style="margin: 10px 0; border-top: 1px solid #2a3a4a; padding-top: 8px;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 6px; color: #88aaff;">ФИЛЬТР ПО ТИПАМ АГЕНТОВ</div>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterExplorer" checked> EXPLORER
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterArchitect" checked> ARCHITECT
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterCoder" checked> CODER
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterGeneral" checked> GENERAL
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterClipping" checked> CLIPPING
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterSearch" checked> SEARCH
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterGraph" checked> GRAPH
                </label>
                <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 4px; cursor: pointer;">
                    <input type="checkbox" id="filterIntegrator" checked> INTEGRATOR
                </label>
                <button id="resetFilters" style="margin-top: 6px; width: 100%; background: #2a3a4a; color: #b7c9e2; border: 1px solid #5a7a8a; padding: 3px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 10px;">Сброс фильтров</button>
            </div>
            
            <div style="margin: 10px 0;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">Яркость связей</div>
                <input type="range" id="edgeBrightness" min="0.2" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.5" style="width: 100%;">
            </div>
            <div style="margin: 10px 0;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">Скорость частиц на связях</div>
                <input type="range" id="particleSpeed" min="0.002" max="0.02" step="0.001" value="0.008" style="width: 100%;">
            </div>
            <div style="margin: 10px 0;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">Скорость восходящих частиц</div>
                <input type="range" id="upwardSpeed" min="0.003" max="0.025" step="0.001" value="0.008" style="width: 100%;">
            </div>
            
            <button id="viewTopBtn" style="width: 100%; background: #1a3a2a; color: #00ff9d; border: 1px solid #00ff9d; padding: 5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 5px;">Вид сверху</button>
            <button id="resetCameraBtn" style="width: 100%; background: #1a2a3a; color: #00ff9d; border: 1px solid #00ff9d; padding: 5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 5px;">Сброс камеры</button>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div id="tooltip" class="agent-tooltip"></div>

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.module.js",
                "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/examples/jsm/"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';
        import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
        import { CSS2DRenderer, CSS2DObject } from 'three/addons/renderers/CSS2DRenderer.js';

        // --- ДАННЫЕ АГЕНТОВ (все Y координаты подняты выше пола) ---
        const agents = [
            { id: 'batuta', name: 'BATUTA', type: 'orchestrator', status: 'active', color: 0xff3366, pos: { x: 0, y: 0.5, z: 0 }, size: 0.9 },
            
            // ПРАВАЯ СТОРОНА (X положительный) - разнесены по Z
            { id: 'explorer_1', name: 'EXPLORER', type: 'explorer', status: 'idle', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 4.2, y: 1.7, z: 2.5 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'coder_1', name: 'CODER', type: 'coder', status: 'idle', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3.8, y: 1.2, z: 1.2 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'clipper_1', name: 'CLIPPER', type: 'clipping', status: 'busy', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 4.5, y: 1.8, z: -1.5 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'analyzer_1', name: 'ANALYZER', type: 'graph', status: 'idle', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 3.2, y: 2.2, z: -3.0 }, size: 0.6 },
            
            // ЛЕВАЯ СТОРОНА (X отрицательный) - разнесены по Z
            { id: 'architect_1', name: 'ARCHITECT', type: 'architect', status: 'busy', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -4.0, y: 2.0, z: 2.8 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'general_1', name: 'GENERAL', type: 'general', status: 'ready', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3.5, y: 1.5, z: 1.3 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'searcher_1', name: 'SEARCHER', type: 'search', status: 'idle', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -4.3, y: 1.9, z: -1.8 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'integrator_1', name: 'INTEGRATOR', type: 'integrator', status: 'ready', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -3.0, y: 1.4, z: -3.2 }, size: 0.6 }
        ];
        
        // Связи: Batuta со всеми агентами
        const links = agents.filter(a => a.id !== 'batuta').map(agent => ({
            source: 'batuta',
            target: agent.id,
            type: 'orchestrates'
        }));
        
        // Дополнительные связи между агентами
        links.push(
            { source: 'architect_1', target: 'coder_1', type: 'assigns' },
            { source: 'coder_1', target: 'general_1', type: 'consults' },
            { source: 'explorer_1', target: 'analyzer_1', type: 'feeds' },
            { source: 'clipper_1', target: 'integrator_1', type: 'sends' },
            { source: 'searcher_1', target: 'explorer_1', type: 'assists' }
        );

        // Сворачивание панели
        const panelHeader = document.getElementById('panelHeader');
        const panelContent = document.getElementById('panelContent');
        const panelToggleIcon = document.getElementById('panelToggleIcon');
        let panelCollapsed = false;

        if (panelHeader && panelContent && panelToggleIcon) {
            let panelCollapsed = false;
            
            panelHeader.addEventListener('click', () => {
                panelCollapsed = !panelCollapsed;
                if (panelCollapsed) {
                    panelContent.style.maxHeight = '0';
                    panelContent.style.padding = '0 15px';
                    panelToggleIcon.style.transform = 'rotate(-90deg)';
                } else {
                    panelContent.style.maxHeight = '500px';
                    panelContent.style.padding = '12px 15px';
                    panelToggleIcon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
                }
            });
        } else {
            console.log('Panel elements not found');
        }
        
        // --- ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ СЦЕНЫ ---
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        // CSS2D рендерер для карточек
        const labelRenderer = new CSS2DRenderer();
        labelRenderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.position = 'absolute';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.top = '0px';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.left = '0px';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        document.body.appendChild(labelRenderer.domElement);
        
        // --- УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ---
        const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
        controls.rotateSpeed = 1.5;
        controls.zoomSpeed = 1.2;
        controls.panSpeed = 0.8;
        controls.enableZoom = true;
        controls.enablePan = true;

        // --- УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ВИДАМИ КАМЕРЫ (правильные оси: Z - высота) ---
        let currentView = 'persp';
        
        function setView(position, target) {
            const startPos = camera.position.clone();
            const startTarget = controls.target.clone();
            const duration = 500;
            const startTime = performance.now();
            
            function animateView(now) {
                const elapsed = now - startTime;
                const t = Math.min(1, elapsed / duration);
                const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
                
                camera.position.lerpVectors(startPos, position, ease);
                controls.target.lerpVectors(startTarget, target, ease);
                controls.update();
                
                if (t < 1) {
                    requestAnimationFrame(animateView);
                }
            }
            
            requestAnimationFrame(animateView);
        }
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            // Вид сверху (смотрим вдоль оси Z вниз)
            const viewTopBtn = document.getElementById('viewTopBtn');
            const viewPerspBtn = document.getElementById('viewPerspBtn');
            const resetViewBtn = document.getElementById('resetViewBtn');
            
            if (viewTopBtn) {
                viewTopBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    // Смотрим сверху: камера наверху по Z, смотрим вниз
                    const target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
                    const position = new THREE.Vector3(0, 20, 0);  // Z = высота
                    setView(position, target);
                    currentView = 'top';
                });
            }
            
            if (viewPerspBtn) {
                viewPerspBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
                    const position = new THREE.Vector3(8, 6, 10);  // X, Y, Z
                    setView(position, target);
                    currentView = 'persp';
                });
            }
            
            if (resetViewBtn) {
                resetViewBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                    const target = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
                    const position = new THREE.Vector3(8, 6, 10);
                    setView(position, target);
                    currentView = 'persp';
                });
            }
        }, 100);     
        
        // --- ОСВЕЩЕНИЕ ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
        scene.add(ambientLight);
        
        const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
        mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
        mainLight.castShadow = true;
        scene.add(mainLight);
        
        const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
        fillLight.position.set(-3, 2, 4);
        scene.add(fillLight);
        
        const backLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffaa44, 0.3);
        backLight.position.set(0, 1, -5);
        scene.add(backLight);
        
        // Неоновое свечение снизу
        const neonLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x00ff9d, 0.5);
        neonLight.position.set(0, -1, 0);
        scene.add(neonLight);
        
        // --- ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ЭЛЕМЕНТЫ ---
        // Сетка
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(18, 50, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a4a6a);
        gridHelper.position.y = -1.0;
        gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
        gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.25;
        scene.add(gridHelper);
        
        // Декоративная окружность на полу (поднята)
        const ringGeometry = new THREE.RingGeometry(2.5, 4.5, 32);
        const ringMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, emissive: 0x00aa55, emissiveIntensity: 0.2, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3 });
        const ringFloor = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeometry, ringMaterial);
        ringFloor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ringFloor.position.y = -0.99;
        scene.add(ringFloor);
        
        // Звездный фон
        const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const starCount = 1200;
        const starPositions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {
            starPositions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 300;
            starPositions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 150;
            starPositions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100 - 50;
        }
        starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));
        const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x88aaff, size: 0.08, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
        const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);
        scene.add(stars);
        
        // --- СОЗДАНИЕ НЕОНОВЫХ КУБИКОВ ---
        const agentsMap = new Map();
        const particleSystems = new Map();
        
        function createNeonCube(agent) {
            const group = new THREE.Group();
            
            // Основной куб
            const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(agent.size, agent.size, agent.size);
            const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                emissive: agent.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                metalness: 0.8,
                roughness: 0.2,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.92
            });
            const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
            cube.castShadow = true;
            group.add(cube);
            
            // Неоновые ребра
            const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
            const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
            const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
            group.add(wireframe);
            
            // Внешнее свечение
            const glowGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(agent.size + 0.12, agent.size + 0.12, agent.size + 0.12);
            const glowMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.15,
                side: THREE.BackSide
            });
            const glowCube = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeometry, glowMaterial);
            group.add(glowCube);
            
            group.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
            
            return group;
        }
        
        // --- CSS2D КАРТОЧКИ ---
        function createAgentCard(agent) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            const colorStyle = '#' + agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
            
            div.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(8,12,25,0.92)';
            div.style.border = `1.5px solid ${colorStyle}`;
            div.style.borderRadius = '8px';
            div.style.padding = '8px 14px';
            div.style.color = '#b7c9e2';
            div.style.fontFamily = "'Share Tech Mono', monospace";
            div.style.fontSize = '11px';
            div.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(8px)';
            div.style.textAlign = 'center';
            div.style.minWidth = '130px';
            div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${colorStyle}40`;
            div.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            div.style.transition = 'all 0.2s';
            
            const statusColor = agent.status === 'idle' ? '#00ff9d' : (agent.status === 'busy' ? '#ff4444' : '#ffaa44');
            const statusText = agent.status === 'idle' ? 'IDLE' : (agent.status === 'busy' ? 'BUSY' : 'READY');
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; color: ${colorStyle}; margin-bottom: 4px;">
                    ${agent.name}
                </div>
                <div style="font-size: 9px; opacity: 0.7;">${agent.type}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 10px; margin-top: 5px;">
                    <span style="color: ${statusColor}">${statusText}</span>
                </div>                
            `;
            
            div.onmouseenter = () => {
                div.style.transform = 'scale(1.05)';
                div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 25px ${colorStyle}`;
                showTooltip(agent);
            };
            div.onmouseleave = () => {
                div.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
                div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${colorStyle}40`;
                hideTooltip();
            };
            
            const label = new CSS2DObject(div);
            label.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y + 1.0, agent.pos.z);
            return label;
        }
        
        // --- СИСТЕМА ЧАСТИЦ (восходящий поток, прямоугольные) ---
        function createParticleSystem(agent) {
            const particleCount = agent.id === 'batuta' ? 28 : 12;
            const particles = [];
            
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
                const radius = agent.size + 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.7;
                const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                const xOffset = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.0;
                const zOffset = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.0;
                
                positions[i*3] = agent.pos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + xOffset * 0.4;
                positions[i*3+1] = agent.pos.y - 0.4 + Math.random() * 1.8;
                positions[i*3+2] = agent.pos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + zOffset * 0.4;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            
            // Прямоугольные частицы (используем квадратные спрайты)
            const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
            canvas.width = 8;
            canvas.height = 8;
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
            ctx.fillStyle = `#${agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0')}`;
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 8, 8);
            ctx.fillStyle = 'white';
            ctx.fillRect(2, 2, 4, 4);
            const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                size: 0.05,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.5,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            const particleSystem = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            
            particleSystem.userData = {
                velocities: Array(particleCount).fill().map(() => 0.006 + Math.random() * 0.01),
                offsets: Array(particleCount).fill().map(() => Math.random() * Math.PI * 2),
                originalY: Array(particleCount).fill().map((_, i) => positions[i*3+1]),
                agentPos: { x: agent.pos.x, y: agent.pos.y, z: agent.pos.z },
                agentSize: agent.size,
                particleCount: particleCount
            };
            
            return particleSystem;
        }
        
        function updateParticles(deltaTime) {
            particleSystems.forEach((particles, agentId) => {
                const data = particles.userData;
                const positions = particles.geometry.attributes.position.array;
                const speedFactor = deltaTime * 60;
                
                for (let i = 0; i < data.particleCount; i++) {
                    let y = positions[i*3+1];
                    y += data.velocities[i] * speedFactor;
                    
                    if (y > data.agentPos.y + 1.3) {
                        y = data.agentPos.y - 0.5;
                        // Обновляем X и Z для эффекта непрерывности
                        const radius = data.agentSize + 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.6;
                        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                        positions[i*3] = data.agentPos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                        positions[i*3+2] = data.agentPos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                    }
                    
                    positions[i*3+1] = y;
                }
                
                particles.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
            });
        }
        
        // --- ИЗОГНУТЫЕ СВЯЗИ (по 2-3 частицы на связь, плавная анимация) ---
        function createCurvedLink(source, target, type, color) {
            const start = new THREE.Vector3(source.pos.x, source.pos.y + 0.2, source.pos.z);
            const end = new THREE.Vector3(target.pos.x, target.pos.y + 0.2, target.pos.z);
            
            const mid = new THREE.Vector3().addVectors(start, end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
            const direction = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(end, start).normalize();
            const perpendicular = new THREE.Vector3(-direction.z, Math.abs(direction.y) + 0.5, direction.x).normalize();
            
            const curveHeight = 1.2;
            mid.x += perpendicular.x * curveHeight;
            mid.y += 1.0;
            mid.z += perpendicular.z * curveHeight;
            
            const curve = new THREE.QuadraticBezierCurve3(start, mid, end);
            const points = curve.getPoints(50);
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
            
            const linkColor = type === 'orchestrates' ? 0x00ff9d : (type === 'assigns' ? 0x3B82F6 : 0xF59E0B);
            const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: linkColor, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5 });
            const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
            
            // ВСЕГО 3 ЧАСТИЦЫ на связь (вместо 15)
            const flowParticles = [];
            const particleCount = 3;
            
            for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
                const particleGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.04, 8, 8);
                const particleMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ 
                    color: linkColor, 
                    emissive: linkColor, 
                    emissiveIntensity: 0.5,
                    transparent: true,
                    opacity: 0.9
                });
                const particle = new THREE.Mesh(particleGeo, particleMat);
                // Разные начальные позиции для равномерного распределения
                particle.userData = { 
                    t: i / particleCount, 
                    speed: 0.0002,  // Медленнее для плавности
                    phase: i * Math.PI * 2 / particleCount
                };
                scene.add(particle);
                flowParticles.push(particle);
            }
            
            return { line, curve, flowParticles, start, end };
        }
        
        // --- ТУЛТИП ---
        const tooltip = document.getElementById('tooltip');
        
        function showTooltip(agent) {
            const colorStyle = '#' + agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
            tooltip.style.display = 'block';
            tooltip.style.borderColor = colorStyle;
            tooltip.innerHTML = `
                <strong style="color: ${colorStyle}">${agent.name}</strong><br>
                Тип: ${agent.type}<br>
                Статус: ${agent.status}<br>
                Скилы: ${agent.id === 'coder_1' ? 'Python, JS, FastAPI' : 
                          agent.id === 'architect_1' ? 'Design, UML, Planning' :
                          agent.id === 'explorer_1' ? 'Discovery, Research' : 'Various'}<br>
                <span style="font-size:9px;">Кликните для задачи</span>
            `;
        }
        
        function hideTooltip() {
            tooltip.style.display = 'none';
        }
        
        function updateTooltipPosition(event) {
            tooltip.style.left = (event.clientX + 15) + 'px';
            tooltip.style.top = (event.clientY - 10) + 'px';
        }
        
        // --- ПОСТРОЕНИЕ ---
        const cubes = [];
        const cards = [];
        const links3d = [];
        
        agents.forEach(agent => {
            const cube = createNeonCube(agent);
            scene.add(cube);
            cubes.push({ cube, agent });
            
            const card = createAgentCard(agent);
            scene.add(card);
            cards.push(card);
            
            const particles = createParticleSystem(agent);
            scene.add(particles);
            particleSystems.set(agent.id, particles);
            
            agentsMap.set(agent.id, agent);
        });
        
        const agentPositions = new Map();
        agents.forEach(a => agentPositions.set(a.id, a));
        
        links.forEach(link => {
            const source = agentPositions.get(link.source);
            const target = agentPositions.get(link.target);
            if (source && target) {
                const curvedLink = createCurvedLink(source, target, link.type);
                scene.add(curvedLink.line);
                links3d.push(curvedLink);
            }
        });
        
        // --- АНИМАЦИЯ ЧАСТИЦ НА СВЯЗЯХ ---
        function updateLinkParticles() {
            links3d.forEach(link => {
                if (!link.flowParticles) return;
                
                link.flowParticles.forEach((particle, idx) => {
                    // Инициализация
                    if (!particle.userData.progress) {
                        particle.userData.progress = idx / link.flowParticles.length;
                        particle.userData.speed = 0.0008 + Math.random() * 0.0004;
                    }
                    
                    // Медленное равномерное движение
                    particle.userData.progress += particle.userData.speed;
                    
                    if (particle.userData.progress >= 1) {
                        particle.userData.progress = 0;
                    }
                    
                    // Движение по кривой
                    if (link.curve) {
                        const point = link.curve.getPoint(particle.userData.progress);
                        particle.position.copy(point);
                    }
                    
                    // Мягкая пульсация
                    const scale = 0.8 + Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.002 + idx) * 0.15;
                    particle.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
                });
            });
        }
        
        // --- АНИМАЦИЯ КУБИКОВ ---
        let time = 0;
        
        function animateCubes(deltaTime) {
            time += deltaTime;
            cubes.forEach(({ cube, agent }) => {
                const intensity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 2) * 0.15;
                cube.children[0].material.emissiveIntensity = agent.status === 'busy' ? intensity + 0.3 : intensity;
                cube.children[2].material.opacity = 0.12 + Math.sin(time * 3) * 0.05;
            });
            
            neonLight.intensity = 0.4 + Math.sin(time * 1.5) * 0.15;
        }
        
        // --- FPS ---
        let frameCount = 0;
        let lastFpsUpdate = performance.now();
        let particlesEnabled = true;
        let animTime = 0;
        
        // --- UI (с проверкой существования кнопок) ---
        const resetCamBtn = document.getElementById('resetCameraBtn');
        if (resetCamBtn) {
            resetCamBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
                controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
                controls.update();
            });
        }
        
        const viewTopBtn = document.getElementById('viewTopBtn');
        if (viewTopBtn) {
            viewTopBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                camera.position.set(0, 0, 20);
                controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
                controls.update();
            });
        }
        
        const toggleBtn = document.getElementById('toggleParticlesBtn');
        if (toggleBtn) {
            toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
                particlesEnabled = !particlesEnabled;
                particleSystems.forEach(ps => { ps.visible = particlesEnabled; });
                toggleBtn.textContent = particlesEnabled ? 'Частицы: ВКЛ' : 'Частицы: ВЫКЛ';
            });
        }
        
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', updateTooltipPosition);
        
        // --- ГЛАВНЫЙ ЦИКЛ ---
        let lastFrameTime = performance.now();
        
        function animate() {
            const now = performance.now();
            let delta = Math.min(0.033, (now - lastFrameTime) / 1000);
            lastFrameTime = now;
            animTime = now;
            
            // FPS счётчик с проверкой
            frameCount++;
            if (now - lastFpsUpdate >= 1000) {
                const fpsElement = document.getElementById('fps');
                if (fpsElement) {
                    fpsElement.textContent = `FPS: ${frameCount}`;
                }
                frameCount = 0;
                lastFpsUpdate = now;
            }
            
            if (particlesEnabled) {
                updateParticles(delta);
            }
            updateLinkParticles(animTime);
            animateCubes(delta);
            
            stars.rotation.y += 0.0005;
            stars.rotation.x += 0.0003;
            ringFloor.rotation.z += 0.002;
            
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
            labelRenderer.render(scene, camera);
            
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        }
        
        animate();
        
        console.log('✅ 3D граф агентов загружен!', agents.length, 'агентов,', links.length, 'связей');
    </script>
</body>
</html>

---

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ru">
<head>

    <title>Агентская сеть - 3D визуализация</title>
    <style>
        body {
            margin: 0;
            overflow: hidden;
            font-family: 'Share Tech Mono', monospace;
            background: #050510;
        }
        
        #info {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            left: 20px;
            color: #00ff9d;
            background: rgba(5,5,20,0.95);
            padding: 12px 20px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            z-index: 1000;
            font-size: 12px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
            pointer-events: none;
            font-family: monospace;
        }
        
        /* Новая панель управления в стиле 3D Graph */
        #control-panel {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            right: 20px;
            background: rgba(10,14,26,0.95);
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            border-radius: 8px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
            z-index: 1000;
            font-family: monospace;
            font-size: 11px;
            color: #b7c9e2;
            min-width: 220px;
            max-width: 260px;
        }
        
        #panelContent {
            padding: 12px 15px;
            transition: max-height 0.3s ease;
            overflow-y: auto;
            overflow-x: hidden;
            max-height: 70vh;
        }
        
        /* Стиль скроллбара */
        #panelContent::-webkit-scrollbar {
            width: 6px;
        }
        
        #panelContent::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
            background: #1a2a3a;
            border-radius: 3px;
        }
        
        #panelContent::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
            background: #00ff9d;
            border-radius: 3px;
        }
        
        /* Фильтры сеткой 2 колонки */
        .filters-grid {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
            gap: 4px 8px;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
        }
        
        .filter-label {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 6px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-size: 10px;
        }
        
        button {
            background: #0a1a2a;
            color: #00ff9d;
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            padding: 6px 14px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-family: monospace;
            transition: all 0.3s;
        }
        
        button:hover {
            background: #00ff9d;
            color: #050510;
        }
        
        .status-badge {
            display: inline-block;
            padding: 2px 6px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            font-size: 10px;
            margin-left: 8px;
        }
        
        .agent-tooltip {
            position: absolute;
            background: #0a1a2a;
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            border-radius: 6px;
            padding: 8px 12px;
            color: #b7c9e2;
            font-size: 11px;
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 2000;
            display: none;
            backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
            font-family: monospace;
        }
        
        input[type="range"] {
            width: 100%;
            background: #1a2a3a;
            height: 2px;
            border-radius: 2px;
            -webkit-appearance: none;
        }
        
        input[type="range"]:focus {
            outline: none;
        }
        
        input[type="range"]::-webkit-slider-thumb {
            -webkit-appearance: none;
            width: 10px;
            height: 10px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: #00ff9d;
            cursor: pointer;
        }
        
        hr {
            border-color: #2a3a4a;
            margin: 8px 0;

    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="control-panel">
        <div id="panelHeader" style="padding: 12px 15px; cursor: pointer; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; color: #00ff9d; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold; border-bottom: 1px solid #2a3a4a;">
            УПРАВЛЕНИЕ <span id="panelToggleIcon" style="transition: transform 0.3s;">▼</span>
        </div>
        <div id="panelContent">
            <div style="margin-bottom: 8px; padding: 4px; background: #1a2a3a; border-radius: 4px; text-align: center;">
                FPS: <span id="fpsValue" style="color: #00ff9d; font-weight: bold;">--</span>
            </div>
            
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleParticles" checked> Восходящие частицы
            </label>
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleStars" checked> Фоновые звезды
            </label>
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleGrid" checked> Сетка
            </label>
            <label style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; margin-bottom: 8px; cursor: pointer;">
                <input type="checkbox" id="toggleLinks" checked> Связи
            </label>
            
            <div style="margin: 10px 0; border-top: 1px solid #2a3a4a; padding-top: 8px;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 6px; color: #88aaff;">ФИЛЬТР ПО ТИПАМ АГЕНТОВ</div>
                <div class="filters-grid">
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterExplorer" checked> EXPLORER
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterArchitect" checked> ARCHITECT
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterCoder" checked> CODER
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterGeneral" checked> GENERAL
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterClipping" checked> CLIPPING
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterSearch" checked> SEARCH
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterGraph" checked> GRAPH
                    </label>
                    <label class="filter-label">
                        <input type="checkbox" id="filterIntegrator" checked> INTEGRATOR
                    </label>
                </div>
                <button id="resetFilters" style="margin-top: 6px; width: 100%; background: #2a3a4a; color: #b7c9e2; border: 1px solid #5a7a8a; padding: 4px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 10px;">Сброс фильтров</button>
            </div>
            
            <div style="margin: 10px 0;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">Яркость связей</div>
                <input type="range" id="edgeBrightness" min="0.2" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.5">
            </div>
            <div style="margin: 10px 0;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">Скорость частиц на связях</div>
                <input type="range" id="particleSpeed" min="0.0002" max="0.002" step="0.0001" value="0.0008">
            </div>
            <div style="margin: 10px 0;">
                <div style="margin-bottom: 4px;">Скорость восходящих частиц</div>
                <input type="range" id="upwardSpeed" min="0.003" max="0.025" step="0.001" value="0.008">
            </div>
            
            <button id="viewTopBtn" style="width: 100%; background: #1a3a2a; color: #00ff9d; border: 1px solid #00ff9d; padding: 5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 5px;">Вид сверху</button>
            <button id="resetCameraBtn" style="width: 100%; background: #1a2a3a; color: #00ff9d; border: 1px solid #00ff9d; padding: 5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 5px;">Сброс камеры</button>
        </div>
    </div>
    <div id="tooltip" class="agent-tooltip"></div>

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.module.js",
                "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/examples/jsm/"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';
        import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
        import { CSS2DRenderer, CSS2DObject } from 'three/addons/renderers/CSS2DRenderer.js';

        // --- ДАННЫЕ АГЕНТОВ ---
        const agents = [
            { id: 'batuta', name: 'BATUTA', type: 'orchestrator', status: 'active', color: 0xff3366, pos: { x: 0, y: 0.5, z: 0 }, size: 0.9 },
            { id: 'explorer_1', name: 'EXPLORER', type: 'explorer', status: 'idle', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 4.2, y: 1.7, z: 2.5 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'coder_1', name: 'CODER', type: 'coder', status: 'idle', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3.8, y: 1.2, z: 1.2 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'clipper_1', name: 'CLIPPER', type: 'clipping', status: 'busy', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 4.5, y: 1.8, z: -1.5 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'analyzer_1', name: 'ANALYZER', type: 'graph', status: 'idle', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 3.2, y: 2.2, z: -3.0 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'architect_1', name: 'ARCHITECT', type: 'architect', status: 'busy', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -4.0, y: 2.0, z: 2.8 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'general_1', name: 'GENERAL', type: 'general', status: 'ready', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3.5, y: 1.5, z: 1.3 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'searcher_1', name: 'SEARCHER', type: 'search', status: 'idle', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -4.3, y: 1.9, z: -1.8 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'integrator_1', name: 'INTEGRATOR', type: 'integrator', status: 'ready', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -3.0, y: 1.4, z: -3.2 }, size: 0.6 }
        ];
        
        // Связи
        const links = agents.filter(a => a.id !== 'batuta').map(agent => ({
            source: 'batuta',
            target: agent.id,
            type: 'orchestrates'
        }));
        
        links.push(
            { source: 'architect_1', target: 'coder_1', type: 'assigns' },
            { source: 'coder_1', target: 'general_1', type: 'consults' },
            { source: 'explorer_1', target: 'analyzer_1', type: 'feeds' },
            { source: 'clipper_1', target: 'integrator_1', type: 'sends' },
            { source: 'searcher_1', target: 'explorer_1', type: 'assists' }
        );

        // --- ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ СЦЕНЫ ---
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        const labelRenderer = new CSS2DRenderer();
        labelRenderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.position = 'absolute';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.top = '0px';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.left = '0px';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        document.body.appendChild(labelRenderer.domElement);
        
        const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
        controls.rotateSpeed = 1.5;
        controls.zoomSpeed = 1.2;
        controls.panSpeed = 0.8;
        controls.enableZoom = true;
        controls.enablePan = true;
        controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
        
        // --- ОСВЕЩЕНИЕ ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
        scene.add(ambientLight);
        
        const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
        mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
        mainLight.castShadow = true;
        scene.add(mainLight);
        
        const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
        fillLight.position.set(-3, 2, 4);
        scene.add(fillLight);
        
        const backLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffaa44, 0.3);
        backLight.position.set(0, 1, -5);
        scene.add(backLight);
        
        const neonLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x00ff9d, 0.5);
        neonLight.position.set(0, -1, 0);
        scene.add(neonLight);
        
        // --- ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ЭЛЕМЕНТЫ ---
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(18, 50, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a4a6a);
        gridHelper.position.y = -1.0;
        gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
        gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.25;
        scene.add(gridHelper);
        
        const ringGeometry = new THREE.RingGeometry(2.5, 4.5, 32);
        const ringMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, emissive: 0x00aa55, emissiveIntensity: 0.2, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3 });
        const ringFloor = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeometry, ringMaterial);
        ringFloor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ringFloor.position.y = -0.99;
        scene.add(ringFloor);
        
        const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const starCount = 1200;
        const starPositions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {
            starPositions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 300;
            starPositions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 150;
            starPositions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100 - 50;
        }
        starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));
        const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x88aaff, size: 0.08, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
        const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);
        scene.add(stars);
        
        // --- СОЗДАНИЕ КУБИКОВ И КАРТОЧЕК ---
        const agentsMap = new Map();
        const particleSystems = new Map();
        const cubes = [];
        const cards = [];
        const links3d = [];
        
        function createNeonCube(agent) {
            const group = new THREE.Group();
            const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(agent.size, agent.size, agent.size);
            const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                emissive: agent.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                metalness: 0.8,
                roughness: 0.2,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.92
            });
            const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
            cube.castShadow = true;
            group.add(cube);
            
            const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
            const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
            const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
            group.add(wireframe);
            
            const glowGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(agent.size + 0.12, agent.size + 0.12, agent.size + 0.12);
            const glowMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.15,
                side: THREE.BackSide
            });
            const glowCube = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeometry, glowMaterial);
            group.add(glowCube);
            
            group.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
            return group;
        }
        
        function createAgentCard(agent) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            const colorStyle = '#' + agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
            
            div.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(8,12,25,0.92)';
            div.style.border = `1.5px solid ${colorStyle}`;
            div.style.borderRadius = '8px';
            div.style.padding = '8px 14px';
            div.style.color = '#b7c9e2';
            div.style.fontFamily = "'Share Tech Mono', monospace";
            div.style.fontSize = '11px';
            div.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(8px)';
            div.style.textAlign = 'center';
            div.style.minWidth = '130px';
            div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${colorStyle}40`;
            div.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            div.style.transition = 'all 0.2s';
            
            const statusColor = agent.status === 'idle' ? '#00ff9d' : (agent.status === 'busy' ? '#ff4444' : '#ffaa44');
            const statusText = agent.status === 'idle' ? 'IDLE' : (agent.status === 'busy' ? 'BUSY' : 'READY');
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; color: ${colorStyle}; margin-bottom: 4px;">${agent.name}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 9px; opacity: 0.7;">${agent.type}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 10px; margin-top: 5px;"><span style="color: ${statusColor}">${statusText}</span></div>
            `;
            
            div.onmouseenter = () => {
                div.style.transform = 'scale(1.05)';
                div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 25px ${colorStyle}`;
                showTooltip(agent);
            };
            div.onmouseleave = () => {
                div.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
                div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${colorStyle}40`;
                hideTooltip();
            };
            
            const label = new CSS2DObject(div);
            label.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y + 1.0, agent.pos.z);
            return label;
        }
        
        function createParticleSystem(agent) {
            const particleCount = agent.id === 'batuta' ? 28 : 12;
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
                const radius = agent.size + 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.7;
                const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                positions[i*3] = agent.pos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;
                positions[i*3+1] = agent.pos.y - 0.4 + Math.random() * 1.8;
                positions[i*3+2] = agent.pos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                size: 0.05,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.5,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            const particleSystem = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            
            particleSystem.userData = {
                velocities: Array(particleCount).fill().map(() => 0.006 + Math.random() * 0.01),
                agentPos: { x: agent.pos.x, y: agent.pos.y, z: agent.pos.z },
                agentSize: agent.size,
                particleCount: particleCount
            };
            
            return particleSystem;
        }
        
        function updateParticles(deltaTime) {
            const speedMultiplier = document.getElementById('upwardSpeed') ? parseFloat(document.getElementById('upwardSpeed').value) : 0.008;
            particleSystems.forEach((particles) => {
                const data = particles.userData;
                const positions = particles.geometry.attributes.position.array;
                const speedFactor = deltaTime * 60 * (speedMultiplier / 0.008);
                
                for (let i = 0; i < data.particleCount; i++) {
                    let y = positions[i*3+1];
                    y += data.velocities[i] * speedFactor;
                    
                    if (y > data.agentPos.y + 1.3) {
                        y = data.agentPos.y - 0.5;
                        const radius = data.agentSize + 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.6;
                        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                        positions[i*3] = data.agentPos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                        positions[i*3+2] = data.agentPos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                    }
                    
                    positions[i*3+1] = y;
                }
                
                particles.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
            });
        }
        
        function createCurvedLink(source, target, type) {
            const start = new THREE.Vector3(source.pos.x, source.pos.y + 0.2, source.pos.z);
            const end = new THREE.Vector3(target.pos.x, target.pos.y + 0.2, target.pos.z);
            
            const mid = new THREE.Vector3().addVectors(start, end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
            const direction = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(end, start).normalize();
            const perpendicular = new THREE.Vector3(-direction.z, Math.abs(direction.y) + 0.5, direction.x).normalize();
            
            mid.x += perpendicular.x * 1.2;
            mid.y += 1.0;
            mid.z += perpendicular.z * 1.2;
            
            const curve = new THREE.QuadraticBezierCurve3(start, mid, end);
            const points = curve.getPoints(50);
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
            
            const linkColor = type === 'orchestrates' ? 0x00ff9d : (type === 'assigns' ? 0x3B82F6 : 0xF59E0B);
            const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: linkColor, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5 });
            const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
            
            const flowParticles = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                const particleGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.04, 8, 8);
                const particleMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: linkColor, emissive: linkColor, emissiveIntensity: 0.5 });
                const particle = new THREE.Mesh(particleGeo, particleMat);
                particle.userData = { progress: i / 3, speed: 0.0008 + Math.random() * 0.0004 };
                scene.add(particle);
                flowParticles.push(particle);
            }
            
            return { line, curve, flowParticles };
        }
        
        function updateLinkParticles() {
            const speedMultiplier = document.getElementById('particleSpeed') ? parseFloat(document.getElementById('particleSpeed').value) : 0.0008;
            links3d.forEach(link => {
                if (!link.flowParticles) return;
                link.flowParticles.forEach((particle, idx) => {
                    particle.userData.progress += particle.userData.speed * (speedMultiplier / 0.0008);
                    if (particle.userData.progress >= 1) particle.userData.progress = 0;
                    if (link.curve) {
                        const point = link.curve.getPoint(particle.userData.progress);
                        particle.position.copy(point);
                    }
                    const scale = 0.8 + Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.002 + idx) * 0.15;
                    particle.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
                });
            });
        }
        
        const tooltip = document.getElementById('tooltip');
        
        function showTooltip(agent) {
            const colorStyle = '#' + agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
            tooltip.style.display = 'block';
            tooltip.style.borderColor = colorStyle;
            tooltip.innerHTML = `
                <strong style="color: ${colorStyle}">${agent.name}</strong><br>
                Тип: ${agent.type}<br>
                Статус: ${agent.status}<br>
                Скилы: ${agent.id === 'coder_1' ? 'Python, JS, FastAPI' : 
                          agent.id === 'architect_1' ? 'Design, UML, Planning' :
                          agent.id === 'explorer_1' ? 'Discovery, Research' : 'Various'}
            `;
        }
        
        function hideTooltip() { tooltip.style.display = 'none'; }
        function updateTooltipPosition(event) {
            tooltip.style.left = (event.clientX + 15) + 'px';
            tooltip.style.top = (event.clientY - 10) + 'px';
        }
        
        // Построение сцены
        agents.forEach(agent => {
            const cube = createNeonCube(agent);
            scene.add(cube);
            cubes.push({ cube, agent });
            
            const card = createAgentCard(agent);
            scene.add(card);
            cards.push(card);
            
            const particles = createParticleSystem(agent);
            scene.add(particles);
            particleSystems.set(agent.id, particles);
            agentsMap.set(agent.id, agent);
        });
        
        const agentPositions = new Map();
        agents.forEach(a => agentPositions.set(a.id, a));
        
        links.forEach(link => {
            const source = agentPositions.get(link.source);
            const target = agentPositions.get(link.target);
            if (source && target) {
                const curvedLink = createCurvedLink(source, target, link.type);
                scene.add(curvedLink.line);
                links3d.push(curvedLink);
            }
        });
        
        // --- UI ОБРАБОТЧИКИ ---
        // Сворачивание панели
        const panelHeader = document.getElementById('panelHeader');
        const panelContent = document.getElementById('panelContent');
        const panelToggleIcon = document.getElementById('panelToggleIcon');
        let panelCollapsed = false;
        
        if (panelHeader) {
            panelHeader.addEventListener('click', () => {
                panelCollapsed = !panelCollapsed;
                if (panelCollapsed) {
                    panelContent.style.maxHeight = '0';
                    panelContent.style.padding = '0 15px';
                    panelToggleIcon.style.transform = 'rotate(-90deg)';
                } else {
                    panelContent.style.maxHeight = '70vh';
                    panelContent.style.padding = '12px 15px';
                    panelToggleIcon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
                }
            });
        }
        
        // Чекбоксы
        document.getElementById('toggleParticles').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            particleSystems.forEach(ps => { ps.visible = e.target.checked; });
        });
        document.getElementById('toggleStars').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            stars.visible = e.target.checked;
        });
        document.getElementById('toggleGrid').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            gridHelper.visible = e.target.checked;
            ringFloor.visible = e.target.checked;
        });
        document.getElementById('toggleLinks').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            links3d.forEach(link => { link.line.visible = e.target.checked; });
        });
        
        // Яркость связей
        document.getElementById('edgeBrightness').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);
            links3d.forEach(link => {
                link.line.material.opacity = val;
            });
        });
        
        // Кнопки
        document.getElementById('viewTopBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            camera.position.set(0, 0, 20);
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            controls.update();
        });
        
        document.getElementById('resetCameraBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            controls.update();
        });
        
        document.getElementById('resetFilters').addEventListener('click', () => {
            const filters = ['filterExplorer', 'filterArchitect', 'filterCoder', 'filterGeneral', 'filterClipping', 'filterSearch', 'filterGraph', 'filterIntegrator'];
            filters.forEach(id => {
                const cb = document.getElementById(id);
                if (cb) cb.checked = true;
            });
        });
        
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', updateTooltipPosition);
        
        // --- АНИМАЦИЯ ---
        let time = 0;
        let frameCount = 0;
        let lastFpsUpdate = performance.now();
        let lastFrameTime = performance.now();
        let particlesVisible = true;
        
        function animateCubes(deltaTime) {
            time += deltaTime;
            cubes.forEach(({ cube, agent }) => {
                const intensity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 2) * 0.15;
                cube.children[0].material.emissiveIntensity = agent.status === 'busy' ? intensity + 0.3 : intensity;
                cube.children[2].material.opacity = 0.12 + Math.sin(time * 3) * 0.05;
            });
            neonLight.intensity = 0.4 + Math.sin(time * 1.5) * 0.15;
        }
        
        function animate() {
            const now = performance.now();
            let delta = Math.min(0.033, (now - lastFrameTime) / 1000);
            lastFrameTime = now;
            
            // FPS счётчик
            frameCount++;
            if (now - lastFpsUpdate >= 1000) {
                const fpsElement = document.getElementById('fpsValue');
                if (fpsElement) fpsElement.textContent = frameCount;
                frameCount = 0;
                lastFpsUpdate = now;
            }
            
            updateParticles(delta);
            updateLinkParticles();
            animateCubes(delta);
            
            stars.rotation.y += 0.0005;
            stars.rotation.x += 0.0003;
            ringFloor.rotation.z += 0.002;
            
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
            labelRenderer.render(scene, camera);
            
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        }
        
        animate();
        console.log('✅ 3D граф агентов загружен!', agents.length, 'агентов,', links.length, 'связей');
    </script>
</body>
</html>
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: КООРДИНАТЫ ВИДА СВЕРХУ

## 📍 Найди в коде строки с обработчиком `viewTopBtn`:

```javascript
document.getElementById('viewTopBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    camera.position.set(0, 0, 20);  // ← ЭТО КООРДИНАТЫ ВИДА СВЕРХУ
    controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
    controls.update();
});
```

## 📊 Что означают координаты:

```javascript
camera.position.set(X, Y, Z);
//                   ↑  ↑  ↑
//                   |  |  └── Z (глубина/высота)
//                   |  └───── Y (горизонталь)
//                   └──────── X (горизонталь)
```

**Вид сверху** - камера смотрит вдоль оси **Z** (которая у нас вертикаль/высота)

- `(0, 0, 20)` - камера прямо над центром (X=0, Y=0), на высоте Z=20
- `(0, 0, 15)` - чуть ниже
- `(0, 0, 25)` - выше (больше обзор)

## 🎯 Как изменить вид сверху:

Хочешь смотреть под другим углом? Измени координаты:

```javascript
// Строгий вид сверху (прямо сверху)
camera.position.set(0, 0, 20);

// Вид сверху-спереди (под углом)
camera.position.set(0, 10, 15);

// Вид сверху-сбоку
camera.position.set(10, 0, 15);

// Вид сверху-изометрия (как в играх)
camera.position.set(10, 10, 15);
```

## 📍 Где находится эта строка в файле:

Она в секции `// --- UI ОБРАБОТЧИКИ ---`, примерно после строки с `resetFilters`. Весь блок:

```javascript
// Кнопки
document.getElementById('viewTopBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
    camera.position.set(0, 0, 20);   // ← МЕНЯЙ ЗДЕСЬ
    controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
    controls.update();
});
```

Просто замени `(0, 0, 20)` на нужные тебе координаты и обнови страницу.

"""
Swarm Center - управление и мониторинг роя агентов
"""

import streamlit as st
import requests

BATUTA_URL = "http://localhost:8010"

def get_agents():
    try:
        resp = requests.get(f"{BATUTA_URL}/api/v1/agents", timeout=5)
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            return resp.json()
        return []
    except Exception as e:
        st.error(f"Batuta error: {e}")
        return []

def render():
    st.markdown("""
    <style>
    .block-container {
        padding-top: 1rem !important;
    }
    /* Компактная карточка */
    .agent-card {
        background: #1a1a2e;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 6px;
        padding: 10px 12px;
        margin-bottom: 12px;
    }
    /* Верхняя строка: имя и статус */
    .agent-header {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
    }
    .agent-name {
        color: #00ff9d;
        font-size: 15px;
        font-weight: bold;
        white-space: nowrap;
        overflow: hidden;
        text-overflow: ellipsis;
        max-width: 130px;
    }
    .agent-status {
        display: inline-block;
        padding: 2px 8px;
        border-radius: 4px;
        font-size: 11px;
        font-weight: bold;
    }
    .status-idle { background: #2a3a4a; color: #8a9bb5; }
    .status-busy { background: #ffaa44; color: #000; }
    /* Вторая строка: ID и вызовы */
    .agent-meta {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        margin-top: 6px;
    }
    .agent-id {
        color: #6a7a8a;
        font-size: 11px;
        font-family: monospace;
    }
    .agent-calls {
        color: #5a6a7a;
        font-size: 11px;
    }
    /* Третья строка: скилы */
    .skills {
        margin-top: 8px;
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 5px;
    }
    .skill-tag {
        background: #0a0a1a;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 3px;
        padding: 2px 6px;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: #8a9bb5;
    }
    .stat-card {
        background: #1a1a2e;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 8px;
        padding: 12px 15px;
        text-align: center;
    }
    .stat-value {
        font-size: 24px;
        font-weight: bold;
        color: #00ff9d;
    }
    .stat-label {
        font-size: 11px;
        color: #8a9bb5;
    }
    .section-title {
        color: #00ff9d;
        font-size: 15px;
        margin: 15px 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: bold;
    }
    .graph-placeholder {
        background: #1a1a2e;
        border: 1px dashed #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 8px;
        height: 400px;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        color: #8a9bb5;
        flex-direction: column;

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: ЗАМЕНЯЕМ ЗАГЛУШКУ НА 3D ГРАФ

## 📍 В файле `swarm_center.py` нужно заменить блок `AGENT NETWORK GRAPH` на iframe с 3D графом.

Вот исправленный файл. Замени содержимое `/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/components/swarm_center.py` на:

```python
"""
Swarm Center - управление и мониторинг роя агентов
"""

import streamlit as st
import requests

BATUTA_URL = "http://localhost:8010"

def get_agents():
    try:
        resp = requests.get(f"{BATUTA_URL}/api/v1/agents", timeout=5)
        if resp.status_code == 200:
            return resp.json()
        return []
    except Exception as e:
        st.error(f"Batuta error: {e}")
        return []

def render():
    st.markdown("""
    <style>
    .block-container {
        padding-top: 1rem !important;
    }
    /* Компактная карточка */
    .agent-card {
        background: #1a1a2e;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 6px;
        padding: 10px 12px;
        margin-bottom: 12px;
    }
    /* Верхняя строка: имя и статус */
    .agent-header {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        align-items: center;
    }
    .agent-name {
        color: #00ff9d;
        font-size: 15px;
        font-weight: bold;
        white-space: nowrap;
        overflow: hidden;
        text-overflow: ellipsis;
        max-width: 130px;
    }
    .agent-status {
        display: inline-block;
        padding: 2px 8px;
        border-radius: 4px;
        font-size: 11px;
        font-weight: bold;
    }
    .status-idle { background: #2a3a4a; color: #8a9bb5; }
    .status-busy { background: #ffaa44; color: #000; }
    /* Вторая строка: ID и вызовы */
    .agent-meta {
        display: flex;
        justify-content: space-between;
        margin-top: 6px;
    }
    .agent-id {
        color: #6a7a8a;
        font-size: 11px;
        font-family: monospace;
    }
    .agent-calls {
        color: #5a6a7a;
        font-size: 11px;
    }
    /* Третья строка: скилы */
    .skills {
        margin-top: 8px;
        display: flex;
        flex-wrap: wrap;
        gap: 5px;
    }
    .skill-tag {
        background: #0a0a1a;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 3px;
        padding: 2px 6px;
        font-size: 11px;
        color: #8a9bb5;
    }
    .stat-card {
        background: #1a1a2e;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 8px;
        padding: 12px 15px;
        text-align: center;
    }
    .stat-value {
        font-size: 24px;
        font-weight: bold;
        color: #00ff9d;
    }
    .stat-label {
        font-size: 11px;
        color: #8a9bb5;
    }
    .section-title {
        color: #00ff9d;
        font-size: 15px;
        margin: 15px 0 10px 0;
        font-weight: bold;
    }
    /* Стиль для iframe 3D графа */
    .graph-container {
        background: #050510;
        border: 1px solid #2a3a4a;
        border-radius: 8px;
        overflow: hidden;
        margin-bottom: 15px;
    }
    .graph-container iframe {
        width: 100%;
        height: 500px;
        border: none;
    }
    .refresh-btn {
        background: #1a2a3a;
        color: #00ff9d;
        border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
        border-radius: 4px;
        padding: 8px 16px;
        cursor: pointer;
        font-family: monospace;
        width: 100%;
    }
    .refresh-btn:hover {
        background: #00ff9d;
        color: #050510;
    }
    </style>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    st.markdown('<div class="section-header" style="font-size: 18px;">SWARM CONTROL CENTER</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    agents = get_agents()
    
    if not agents:
        st.warning("No agents found. Make sure Batuta is running.")
        return
    
    total = len(agents)
    busy = len([a for a in agents if a.get('status') == 'busy'])
    idle = len([a for a in agents if a.get('status') == 'idle'])
    total_calls = sum(a.get('metrics', {}).get('calls', 0) for a in agents)
    
    col1, col2, col3, col4 = st.columns(4)
    with col1:
        st.markdown(f'<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-value">{total}</div><div class="stat-label">AGENTS</div></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    with col2:
        st.markdown(f'<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-value" style="color: #ffaa44;">{busy}</div><div class="stat-label">BUSY</div></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    with col3:
        st.markdown(f'<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-value">{idle}</div><div class="stat-label">IDLE</div></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    with col4:
        st.markdown(f'<div class="stat-card"><div class="stat-value">{total_calls}</div><div class="stat-label">CALLS</div></div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    st.markdown('<div class="section-title">AGENTS DIRECTORY</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    skills_map = {
        "explorer": ["graph_analysis", "search", "node_discovery"],
        "architect": ["diagram_generation", "design", "plantuml"],
        "coder": ["code_generation", "refactoring", "debugging"],
        "general": ["chat", "coordination", "hitl"],
        "clipping": ["fetch_clipping", "parse_article"],
        "search": ["vector_search", "semantic_search"],
        "graph": ["cypher_query", "relationship_mining"],
        "integrator": ["merge_from_sandbox", "apply_patch", "rollback"]
    }
    
    rows = [agents[i:i+4] for i in range(0, len(agents), 4)]
    
    for row in rows:
        cols = st.columns(4)
        for i, agent in enumerate(row):
            with cols[i]:
                agent_id = agent.get('id', 'unknown')
                name = agent.get('name', 'Unknown')
                agent_type = agent.get('type', 'unknown')
                status = agent.get('status', 'unknown')
                calls = agent.get('metrics', {}).get('calls', 0)
                
                status_class = "status-idle"
                if status == "busy":
                    status_class = "status-busy"
                
                skills = skills_map.get(agent_type, [])
                skills_html = ''.join(f'<span class="skill-tag">{s}</span>' for s in skills[:3])
                
                st.markdown(f"""
                <div class="agent-card">
                    <div class="agent-header">
                        <span class="agent-name">{name}</span>
                        <span class="agent-status {status_class}">{status.upper()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="agent-meta">
                        <span class="agent-id">{agent_id}</span>
                        <span class="agent-calls">calls: {calls}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="skills">{skills_html}</div>
                </div>
                """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    st.markdown('<div class="section-title">AGENT NETWORK GRAPH</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    # 3D ГРАФ вместо заглушки
    st.markdown(f"""
    <div class="graph-container">
        <iframe src="http://localhost:8085/agents_3d_demo.html"></iframe>
    </div>
    """, unsafe_allow_html=True)
    
    col_refresh, col_empty = st.columns([1, 3])
    with col_refresh:
        if st.button("⟳ REFRESH GRAPH", use_container_width=True):
            st.rerun()
```

```python
    <div class="graph-container">

```

```python
    st.components.v1.iframe(

        height=500,
        scrolling=False
    )
```

## 🔍 Открой консоль браузера в командном центре (F12) и посмотри ошибки.

```bash
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>

    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="info">3D AGENT NETWORK | Batuta → Agents</div>
    
    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.module.js",
                "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/examples/jsm/"
            }

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';
```
