```bash
        // Агенты
        const agents = [
            { id: 'batuta', name: 'BATUTA', color: 0xff3366, pos: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 } },
            { id: 'explorer', name: 'EXPLORER', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 3, y: 1.5, z: 2 } },
            { id: 'architect', name: 'ARCHITECT', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -3, y: 1.5, z: 2 } },
            { id: 'coder', name: 'CODER', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3, y: -1, z: 2 } },
            { id: 'general', name: 'GENERAL', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3, y: -1, z: 2 } },
            { id: 'clipper', name: 'CLIPPER', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 3, y: 0.5, z: -2 } },
            { id: 'searcher', name: 'SEARCHER', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -3, y: 0.5, z: -2 } },
            { id: 'analyzer', name: 'ANALYZER', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 1.5, y: 2, z: -2.5 } },
            { id: 'integrator', name: 'INTEGRATOR', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -1.5, y: -1.8, z: -2.5 } }
        ];
        
        // Сцены
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.02);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
        document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
        controls.autoRotate = true;
        controls.autoRotateSpeed = 1;
        controls.enableZoom = true;
        controls.enablePan = true;
        
        // Освещение
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
        scene.add(ambientLight);
        const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
        mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
        scene.add(mainLight);
        const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
        fillLight.position.set(-2, 1, 3);
        scene.add(fillLight);
        
        // Сетка
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(15, 20, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a3a4a);
        gridHelper.position.y = -1.5;
        gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
        gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.3;
        scene.add(gridHelper);
        
        // Создание узлов
        const nodes = [];
        agents.forEach(agent => {
            const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.7, 0.7, 0.7);
            const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                emissive: agent.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                metalness: 0.7,
                roughness: 0.3
            });
            const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
            cube.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
            scene.add(cube);
            nodes.push(cube);
            
            // Ребра
            const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
            const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
            const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
            cube.add(wireframe);
        });
        
        // Связи Batuta со всеми
        const batuta = agents.find(a => a.id === 'batuta');
        const otherAgents = agents.filter(a => a.id !== 'batuta');
        
        otherAgents.forEach(agent => {
            const start = new THREE.Vector3(batuta.pos.x, batuta.pos.y + 0.2, batuta.pos.z);
            const end = new THREE.Vector3(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y + 0.2, agent.pos.z);
            const points = [start, end];
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
            const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
            const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
            scene.add(line);
        });
        
        // Анимация
        function animate() {
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
        }
        animate();
        
        console.log('3D Graph loaded');
    </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: ПРИЧИНА НАЙДЕНА!

Из диагностики:
1. ✅ Граф работает отдельно (открывается напрямую)
2. ✅ Сервер 8085 работает и отдает файл (видно `GET /agents_3d_demo.html HTTP/1.1 200`)
3. ✅ Three.js загружается
4. ❌ **Нет ошибок в консоли** - это странно

## 🔍 Проблема: Streamlit iframe может не отображать содержимое из-за политик безопасности

### Решение: Добавь заголовки для iframe в HTTP сервер

Останови текущий сервер 8085 и запусти с правильными заголовками:

```bash
# Остановить текущий сервер
kill -9 89969

# Запустить сервер с правильными CORS заголовками
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/app/static
python3 -c "
from http.server import HTTPServer, SimpleHTTPRequestHandler
class CORSRequestHandler(SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):
    def end_headers(self):
        self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*')
        self.send_header('X-Frame-Options', 'ALLOWALL')
        super().end_headers()
HTTPServer(('0.0.0.0', 8085), CORSRequestHandler).serve_forever()
" &
```

### Или более простой способ - используй полный URL в iframe:

В `swarm_center.py` замени:

```python
st.components.v1.iframe(
    src="http://localhost:8085/agents_3d_demo.html",
    height=550,
    scrolling=False
)
```

**На:**

```python
st.components.v1.iframe(
    src="http://127.0.0.1:8085/agents_3d_demo.html",
    height=550,
    scrolling=False
)
```

### Или используй html с прямым встраиванием:

```python
with open('/Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/app/static/agents_3d_demo.html', 'r') as f:
    html_content = f.read()
    st.components.v1.html(html_content, height=600, scrolling=False)
```

**Попробуй третий вариант - он самый надежный.** Он не использует iframe, а встраивает HTML напрямую.

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: ПРОБЛЕМА С ЗАГРУЗКОЙ В STREAMLIT

## 🔍 Проблема: Streamlit конфликтует с Three.js из-за динамической загрузки скриптов

Решение - добавить задержку и проверку готовности DOM.

## 📍 Создай адаптированную версию для Streamlit:

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/app/static/agents_3d_streamlit.html << 'EOF'
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>3D Agent Graph</title>
    <style>
        body { margin: 0; overflow: hidden; font-family: monospace; background: #050510; }
        #status { 
            position: absolute; 
            bottom: 10px; 
            left: 10px; 
            color: #00ff9d; 
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5); 
            padding: 2px 6px; 
            border-radius: 3px; 
            font-size: 10px;
            z-index: 1000;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="status">Loading 3D Graph...</div>
    
    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.module.js",
                "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/examples/jsm/"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        // Ждем загрузки DOM
        function init() {
            document.getElementById('status').textContent = 'Initializing...';
            
            import * as THREE from 'three';
            import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
            
            const agents = [
                { id: 'batuta', name: 'BATUTA', color: 0xff3366, pos: { x: 0, y: 0.5, z: 0 } },
                { id: 'explorer', name: 'EXPLORER', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 4.2, y: 1.7, z: 2.5 } },
                { id: 'architect', name: 'ARCHITECT', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -4.0, y: 2.0, z: 2.8 } },
                { id: 'coder', name: 'CODER', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3.8, y: 1.2, z: 1.2 } },
                { id: 'general', name: 'GENERAL', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3.5, y: 1.5, z: 1.3 } },
                { id: 'clipper', name: 'CLIPPER', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 4.5, y: 1.8, z: -1.5 } },
                { id: 'searcher', name: 'SEARCHER', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -4.3, y: 1.9, z: -1.8 } },
                { id: 'analyzer', name: 'ANALYZER', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 3.2, y: 2.2, z: -3.0 } },
                { id: 'integrator', name: 'INTEGRATOR', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -3.0, y: 1.4, z: -3.2 } }
            ];
            
            const scene = new THREE.Scene();
            scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
            scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);
            
            const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
            camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
            
            const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
            renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
            document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
            
            const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
            controls.enableDamping = true;
            controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
            controls.autoRotate = true;
            controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.8;
            controls.enableZoom = true;
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            
            // Освещение
            const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
            scene.add(ambientLight);
            const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
            mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
            scene.add(mainLight);
            const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
            fillLight.position.set(-2, 1, 3);
            scene.add(fillLight);
            
            // Сетка
            const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(18, 30, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a3a4a);
            gridHelper.position.y = -1.0;
            gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
            gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.25;
            scene.add(gridHelper);
            
            // Узлы
            agents.forEach(agent => {
                const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.65, 0.65, 0.65);
                const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                    color: agent.color,
                    emissive: agent.color,
                    emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                    metalness: 0.7,
                    roughness: 0.3
                });
                const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
                cube.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
                scene.add(cube);
                
                const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
                const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
                const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
                cube.add(wireframe);
            });
            
            // Связи
            const batuta = agents.find(a => a.id === 'batuta');
            agents.filter(a => a.id !== 'batuta').forEach(agent => {
                const points = [
                    new THREE.Vector3(batuta.pos.x, batuta.pos.y + 0.2, batuta.pos.z),
                    new THREE.Vector3(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y + 0.2, agent.pos.z)
                ];
                const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
                const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
                const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
                scene.add(line);
            });
            
            document.getElementById('status').textContent = 'Ready | Mouse drag to rotate';
            setTimeout(() => {
                const statusDiv = document.getElementById('status');
                if (statusDiv) statusDiv.style.opacity = '0.5';
            }, 3000);
            
            function animate() {
                requestAnimationFrame(animate);
                controls.update();
                renderer.render(scene, camera);
            }
            animate();
            
            // Адаптация к размеру окна
            window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
                camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
                camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
                renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
            });
        }
        
        // Запускаем после загрузки страницы
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
        } else {
            init();
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: SCRIPTS НЕ ЗАГРУЖАЮТСЯ В STREAMLIT

Проблема: Streamlit блокирует `import map` и динамическую загрузку модулей.

## 🔧 Решение: Создай standalone версию с полным кодом без import map

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/app/static/agents_3d_standalone.html << 'EOF'
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>3D Agent Graph</title>
    <style>
        body { margin: 0; overflow: hidden; font-family: monospace; background: #050510; }
        #status { 
            position: absolute; 
            bottom: 10px; 
            left: 10px; 
            color: #00ff9d; 
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.7); 
            padding: 4px 8px; 
            border-radius: 4px; 
            font-size: 11px;
            z-index: 1000;
            font-family: monospace;
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <div id="status">Loading Three.js...</div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/controls/OrbitControls.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        // Ждем загрузки Three.js
        function init() {
            document.getElementById('status').textContent = 'Initializing 3D Graph...';
            
            // Агенты
            const agents = [
                { id: 'batuta', name: 'BATUTA', color: 0xff3366, pos: { x: 0, y: 0.5, z: 0 } },
                { id: 'explorer', name: 'EXPLORER', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 4.2, y: 1.7, z: 2.5 } },
                { id: 'architect', name: 'ARCHITECT', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -4.0, y: 2.0, z: 2.8 } },
                { id: 'coder', name: 'CODER', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3.8, y: 1.2, z: 1.2 } },
                { id: 'general', name: 'GENERAL', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3.5, y: 1.5, z: 1.3 } },
                { id: 'clipper', name: 'CLIPPER', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 4.5, y: 1.8, z: -1.5 } },
                { id: 'searcher', name: 'SEARCHER', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -4.3, y: 1.9, z: -1.8 } },
                { id: 'analyzer', name: 'ANALYZER', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 3.2, y: 2.2, z: -3.0 } },
                { id: 'integrator', name: 'INTEGRATOR', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -3.0, y: 1.4, z: -3.2 } }
            ];
            
            // Сцена
            const scene = new THREE.Scene();
            scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
            scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);
            
            // Камера
            const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
            camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
            camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
            
            // Рендерер
            const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
            renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
            document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
            
            // Управление
            const controls = new THREE.OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
            controls.enableDamping = true;
            controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
            controls.autoRotate = true;
            controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.8;
            controls.enableZoom = true;
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            
            // Освещение
            const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
            scene.add(ambientLight);
            
            const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
            mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
            scene.add(mainLight);
            
            const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
            fillLight.position.set(-2, 1, 3);
            scene.add(fillLight);
            
            const backLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffaa44, 0.2);
            backLight.position.set(0, 1, -5);
            scene.add(backLight);
            
            // Сетка
            const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(18, 30, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a3a4a);
            gridHelper.position.y = -1.0;
            gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
            gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.25;
            scene.add(gridHelper);
            
            // Узлы (кубики)
            agents.forEach(agent => {
                const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.65, 0.65, 0.65);
                const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                    color: agent.color,
                    emissive: agent.color,
                    emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                    metalness: 0.7,
                    roughness: 0.3
                });
                const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
                cube.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
                scene.add(cube);
                
                // Ребра
                const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
                const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
                const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
                cube.add(wireframe);
                
                // Свечение
                const glowGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.77, 0.77, 0.77);
                const glowMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
                    color: agent.color,
                    transparent: true,
                    opacity: 0.15
                });
                const glowCube = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeometry, glowMaterial);
                cube.add(glowCube);
            });
            
            // Связи от Batuta
            const batuta = agents.find(a => a.id === 'batuta');
            agents.filter(a => a.id !== 'batuta').forEach(agent => {
                const points = [
                    new THREE.Vector3(batuta.pos.x, batuta.pos.y + 0.2, batuta.pos.z),
                    new THREE.Vector3(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y + 0.2, agent.pos.z)
                ];
                const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
                const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
                const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
                scene.add(line);
            });
            
            // Частицы фона
            const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const starCount = 1000;
            const starPositions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);
            for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {
                starPositions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
                starPositions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100;
                starPositions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 80 - 40;
            }
            starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));
            const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x88aaff, size: 0.08, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
            const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);
            scene.add(stars);
            
            let time = 0;
            document.getElementById('status').textContent = '3D Graph Ready | Drag to rotate';
            setTimeout(() => {
                const s = document.getElementById('status');
                if (s) s.style.opacity = '0.5';
            }, 4000);
            
            // Анимация
            function animate() {
                requestAnimationFrame(animate);
                time += 0.01;
                controls.update();
                stars.rotation.y += 0.0005;
                stars.rotation.x += 0.0003;
                renderer.render(scene, camera);
            }
            animate();
            
            // Адаптация к размеру
            window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
                camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
                camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
                renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
            });
        }
        
        // Проверяем загрузку Three.js
        if (typeof THREE !== 'undefined') {
            init();
        } else {
            window.addEventListener('load', () => {
                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (typeof THREE !== 'undefined') {
                        init();
                    } else {
                        document.getElementById('status').textContent = 'ERROR: Three.js failed to load';
                    }
                }, 500);
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: ПОЛНАЯ ВЕРСИЯ С ПЕНДУЛЯРНЫМ ВРАЩЕНИЕМ

```bash
cat > /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/tactical-command/app/static/agents_3d_standalone.html << 'EOF'
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>3D Agent Graph - UCH Swarm</title>
    <style>
        body { margin: 0; overflow: hidden; font-family: 'Share Tech Mono', monospace; background: #050510; }
        #status { 
            position: absolute; 
            bottom: 15px; 
            left: 15px; 
            color: #00ff9d; 
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.6); 
            padding: 6px 12px; 
            border-radius: 4px; 
            font-size: 11px;
            z-index: 1000;
            font-family: monospace;
            pointer-events: none;
            backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
        }
        #controls-hint {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 15px;
            right: 15px;
            color: #6a7a8a;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            padding: 4px 8px;

            background: rgba(10,14,26,0.9);
            border: 1px solid #00ff9d;
            color: #00ff9d;
            padding: 6px 12px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-family: monospace;
            font-size: 11px;
            z-index: 1000;
            transition: all 0.3s;
        }
        .view-btn:hover {
            background: #00ff9d;
            color: #050510;
        }
        .pendulum-toggle {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            right: 120px;
            background: rgba(10,14,26,0.9);
            border: 1px solid #8B5CF6;
            color: #8B5CF6;
            padding: 6px 12px;

            transition: all 0.3s;

            background: #8B5CF6;
            color: #050510;
            border-color: #8B5CF6;

        .pendulum-toggle:hover {

</head>
<body>
    <div id="status">Loading 3D Agent Graph...</div>
    <div id="controls-hint">🖱️ ЛКМ вращение | ПКМ панорама | Колесо зум</div>
    <button class="view-btn" id="resetView">СБРОС КАМЕРЫ</button>
    <button class="view-btn" id="topView" style="right: 130px;">ВИД СВЕРХУ</button>
    <button class="pendulum-toggle" id="pendulumToggle">🌊 ПЕНДУЛЮМ</button>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/three@0.128.0/examples/js/controls/OrbitControls.js"></script>
    
    <script>
        // ========== ДАННЫЕ АГЕНТОВ ==========
        const agents = [

            { id: 'explorer', name: 'EXPLORER', type: 'explorer', status: 'idle', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 4.2, y: 1.7, z: 2.5 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'architect', name: 'ARCHITECT', type: 'architect', status: 'busy', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -4.0, y: 2.0, z: 2.8 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'coder', name: 'CODER', type: 'coder', status: 'idle', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3.8, y: 1.2, z: 1.2 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'general', name: 'GENERAL', type: 'general', status: 'ready', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3.5, y: 1.5, z: 1.3 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'clipper', name: 'CLIPPER', type: 'clipping', status: 'busy', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 4.5, y: 1.8, z: -1.5 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'searcher', name: 'SEARCHER', type: 'search', status: 'idle', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -4.3, y: 1.9, z: -1.8 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'analyzer', name: 'ANALYZER', type: 'graph', status: 'idle', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 3.2, y: 2.2, z: -3.0 }, size: 0.65 },
            { id: 'integrator', name: 'INTEGRATOR', type: 'integrator', status: 'ready', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -3.0, y: 1.4, z: -3.2 }, size: 0.65 }
        ];
        
        // Связи
        const links = [
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'explorer', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'architect', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'coder', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'general', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'clipper', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'searcher', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'analyzer', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'batuta', target: 'integrator', type: 'orchestrates' },
            { source: 'architect', target: 'coder', type: 'assigns' },
            { source: 'coder', target: 'general', type: 'consults' },
            { source: 'explorer', target: 'analyzer', type: 'feeds' },
            { source: 'clipper', target: 'integrator', type: 'sends' },
            { source: 'searcher', target: 'explorer', type: 'assists' }
        ];
        
        // Цвета для типов связей
        const linkColors = {
            orchestrates: 0x00ff9d,
            assigns: 0x3B82F6,
            consults: 0xF59E0B,
            feeds: 0xEC4899,
            sends: 0x14B8A6,
            assists: 0x8B5CF6
        };
        
        // ========== ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ==========
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.012);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        const controls = new THREE.OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
        controls.rotateSpeed = 1.2;
        controls.zoomSpeed = 1.2;
        controls.panSpeed = 0.8;
        controls.enableZoom = true;
        controls.enablePan = true;
        controls.autoRotate = false;
        controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
        
        // ========== ОСВЕЩЕНИЕ ==========
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
        scene.add(ambientLight);
        
        const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
        mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
        mainLight.castShadow = true;
        scene.add(mainLight);
        
        const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
        fillLight.position.set(-3, 2, 4);
        scene.add(fillLight);
        
        const backLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffaa44, 0.3);
        backLight.position.set(0, 1, -5);
        scene.add(backLight);
        
        const neonLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x00ff9d, 0.4);
        neonLight.position.set(0, -1, 0);
        scene.add(neonLight);
        
        // ========== ДЕКОРАТИВНЫЕ ЭЛЕМЕНТЫ ==========
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(18, 30, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a3a4a);
        gridHelper.position.y = -1.0;
        gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
        gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.25;
        scene.add(gridHelper);
        
        const ringGeometry = new THREE.RingGeometry(2.5, 4.5, 32);
        const ringMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, emissive: 0x00aa55, emissiveIntensity: 0.2, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3 });
        const ringFloor = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeometry, ringMaterial);
        ringFloor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ringFloor.position.y = -0.99;
        scene.add(ringFloor);
        
        // Звезды
        const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const starCount = 1500;
        const starPositions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {
            starPositions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 300;
            starPositions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 150;
            starPositions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100 - 50;
        }
        starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));
        const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x88aaff, size: 0.07, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
        const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);
        scene.add(stars);
        
        // ========== СОЗДАНИЕ УЗЛОВ ==========
        const agentMap = new Map();
        const cubes = [];
        
        agents.forEach(agent => {
            const group = new THREE.Group();
            const size = agent.size || 0.65;
            const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size, size, size);
            const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                emissive: agent.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                metalness: 0.8,
                roughness: 0.2,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.92
            });
            const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
            cube.castShadow = true;
            group.add(cube);
            
            // Неоновые ребра
            const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
            const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
            const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
            group.add(wireframe);
            
            // Свечение
            const glowGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(size + 0.12, size + 0.12, size + 0.12);
            const glowMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.15,
                side: THREE.BackSide
            });
            const glowCube = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeometry, glowMaterial);
            group.add(glowCube);
            
            group.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
            scene.add(group);
            
            agentMap.set(agent.id, { group, agent });
            cubes.push({ group, agent });
        });
        
        // ========== СОЗДАНИЕ СВЯЗЕЙ ==========
        const agentPositions = new Map();
        agents.forEach(a => agentPositions.set(a.id, a));
        
        links.forEach(link => {
            const source = agentPositions.get(link.source);
            const target = agentPositions.get(link.target);
            if (source && target) {
                const start = new THREE.Vector3(source.pos.x, source.pos.y + 0.2, source.pos.z);
                const end = new THREE.Vector3(target.pos.x, target.pos.y + 0.2, target.pos.z);
                
                // Изогнутая линия
                const mid = new THREE.Vector3().addVectors(start, end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
                const direction = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(end, start).normalize();
                const perpendicular = new THREE.Vector3(-direction.z, Math.abs(direction.y) + 0.5, direction.x).normalize();
                mid.x += perpendicular.x * 1.0;
                mid.y += 0.8;
                mid.z += perpendicular.z * 1.0;
                
                const curve = new THREE.QuadraticBezierCurve3(start, mid, end);
                const points = curve.getPoints(50);
                const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
                const color = linkColors[link.type] || 0x888888;
                const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: color, transparent: true, opacity: 0.45 });
                const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
                scene.add(line);
                
                // Частицы на связях (3 штуки)
                for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                    const particleGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.05, 6, 6);
                    const particleMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: color, emissive: color, emissiveIntensity: 0.5 });
                    const particle = new THREE.Mesh(particleGeo, particleMat);
                    particle.userData = { curve: curve, progress: i / 3, speed: 0.0006 + Math.random() * 0.0003 };
                    scene.add(particle);
                }
            }
        });
        
        // Сбор всех частиц связей для анимации
        const linkParticles = [];
        scene.children.forEach(child => {
            if (child.isMesh && child.geometry.type === 'SphereGeometry' && child.userData.curve) {
                linkParticles.push(child);
            }
        });
        
        // ========== СИСТЕМА ЧАСТИЦ ВОКРУГ КУБОВ ==========
        const particleSystems = [];
        
        agents.forEach(agent => {
            const particleCount = agent.id === 'batuta' ? 35 : 18;
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
                const radius = (agent.size || 0.65) + 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.6;
                const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                positions[i*3] = agent.pos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                positions[i*3+1] = agent.pos.y - 0.3 + Math.random() * 1.6;
                positions[i*3+2] = agent.pos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                size: 0.05,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.6,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            const particleSystem = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            particleSystem.userData = {
                velocities: Array(particleCount).fill().map(() => 0.005 + Math.random() * 0.008),
                agentPos: { x: agent.pos.x, y: agent.pos.y, z: agent.pos.z },
                agentSize: agent.size || 0.65,
                particleCount: particleCount
            };
            scene.add(particleSystem);
            particleSystems.push(particleSystem);
        });
        
        // ========== ПЕНДУЛЯРНОЕ ВРАЩЕНИЕ ==========
        let pendulumEnabled = false;
        let pendulumAngle = 0;
        let pendulumDirection = 1;
        let pendulumSpeed = 0.002;
        let baseCameraPos = { x: 8, y: 6, z: 10 };
        let pendulumOffset = { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 };
        
        function updatePendulum() {
            if (!pendulumEnabled) return;
            
            pendulumAngle += pendulumSpeed * pendulumDirection;
            if (pendulumAngle > Math.PI / 3) {
                pendulumAngle = Math.PI / 3;
                pendulumDirection = -1;
            } else if (pendulumAngle < -Math.PI / 3) {
                pendulumAngle = -Math.PI / 3;
                pendulumDirection = 1;
            }
            
            // Плавное движение камеры по дуге
            const radius = 12;
            const centerX = 0;
            const centerZ = 0;
            const angleRad = pendulumAngle;
            
            const xOffset = Math.sin(angleRad) * 3;
            const zOffset = Math.cos(angleRad) * 2 - 2;
            
            camera.position.x = baseCameraPos.x + xOffset;
            camera.position.z = baseCameraPos.z + zOffset;
            camera.position.y = baseCameraPos.y + Math.abs(Math.sin(angleRad)) * 1.5;
            
            controls.target.set(0, 0.5, 0);
            controls.update();
        }
        
        // ========== АНИМАЦИЯ ==========
        let time = 0;
        let lastTime = performance.now();
        
        function animateParticles(deltaTime) {
            const speedFactor = deltaTime * 60;
            particleSystems.forEach(ps => {
                const data = ps.userData;
                const positions = ps.geometry.attributes.position.array;
                
                for (let i = 0; i < data.particleCount; i++) {
                    let y = positions[i*3+1];
                    y += data.velocities[i] * speedFactor;
                    
                    if (y > data.agentPos.y + 1.2) {
                        y = data.agentPos.y - 0.5;
                        const radius = data.agentSize + 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.6;
                        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                        positions[i*3] = data.agentPos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                        positions[i*3+2] = data.agentPos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                    }
                    positions[i*3+1] = y;
                }
                ps.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
            });
        }
        
        function animateLinkParticles() {
            linkParticles.forEach(particle => {
                particle.userData.progress += particle.userData.speed;
                if (particle.userData.progress >= 1) {
                    particle.userData.progress = 0;
                }
                const point = particle.userData.curve.getPoint(particle.userData.progress);
                particle.position.copy(point);
                
                const scale = 0.7 + Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.003) * 0.2;
                particle.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
            });
        }
        
        function animateCubes(deltaTime) {
            time += deltaTime;
            cubes.forEach(({ group, agent }) => {
                const cube = group.children[0];
                const glow = group.children[2];
                const intensity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 2.5) * 0.15;
                cube.material.emissiveIntensity = agent.status === 'busy' ? intensity + 0.3 : intensity;
                if (glow && glow.material) glow.material.opacity = 0.12 + Math.sin(time * 3) * 0.05;
            });
            neonLight.intensity = 0.4 + Math.sin(time * 1.5) * 0.15;
        }
        
        function animate() {
            const now = performance.now();
            let delta = Math.min(0.033, (now - lastTime) / 1000);
            lastTime = now;
            
            animateParticles(delta);
            animateLinkParticles();
            animateCubes(delta);
            updatePendulum();
            
            stars.rotation.y += 0.0003;
            stars.rotation.x += 0.0002;
            ringFloor.rotation.z += 0.001;
            
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        }
        
        // ========== UI ОБРАБОТЧИКИ ==========
        document.getElementById('resetView').addEventListener('click', () => {
            camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
            controls.target.set(0, 0.5, 0);
            controls.update();
            if (pendulumEnabled) {
                pendulumEnabled = false;
                document.getElementById('pendulumToggle').classList.remove('active');
                document.getElementById('pendulumToggle').textContent = '🌊 ПЕНДУЛЮМ';
            }
        });
        
        document.getElementById('topView').addEventListener('click', () => {
            camera.position.set(0, 0, 18);
            controls.target.set(0, 0.5, 0);
            controls.update();
            if (pendulumEnabled) {
                pendulumEnabled = false;
                document.getElementById('pendulumToggle').classList.remove('active');
                document.getElementById('pendulumToggle').textContent = '🌊 ПЕНДУЛЮМ';
            }
        });
        
        const pendulumBtn = document.getElementById('pendulumToggle');
        pendulumBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            pendulumEnabled = !pendulumEnabled;
            if (pendulumEnabled) {
                baseCameraPos = { x: camera.position.x, y: camera.position.y, z: camera.position.z };
                pendulumAngle = 0;
                pendulumDirection = 1;
                pendulumBtn.classList.add('active');
                pendulumBtn.textContent = '⏸️ ПЕНДУЛЮМ';
                controls.autoRotate = false;
            } else {
                pendulumBtn.classList.remove('active');
                pendulumBtn.textContent = '🌊 ПЕНДУЛЮМ';
                camera.position.set(baseCameraPos.x, baseCameraPos.y, baseCameraPos.z);
                controls.target.set(0, 0.5, 0);
                controls.update();
            }
        });
        
        // Статус
        document.getElementById('status').innerHTML = '✅ 3D Agent Graph | ' + agents.length + ' agents | ' + links.length + ' connections';
        setTimeout(() => {
            const s = document.getElementById('status');
            if (s) s.style.opacity = '0.6';
        }, 5000);
        
        // Адаптация к размеру окна
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            camera.aspect = window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight;
            camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
            renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        });
        
        // Запуск
        animate();
        console.log('3D Agent Graph loaded');
    </script>
</body>
</html>
EOF
```

            <button id="resetCameraBtn" style="width: 100%; background: #1a2a3a; color: #00ff9d; border: 1px solid #00ff9d; padding: 5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 4px; margin-top: 5px;">Сброс камеры</button>

    <script type="importmap">
        {
            "imports": {
                "three": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.module.js",
                "three/addons/": "https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/examples/jsm/"
            }
        }
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        import * as THREE from 'three';
        import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';
        import { CSS2DRenderer, CSS2DObject } from 'three/addons/renderers/CSS2DRenderer.js';

        // --- ДАННЫЕ АГЕНТОВ ---
        const agents = [
            { id: 'batuta', name: 'BATUTA', type: 'orchestrator', status: 'active', color: 0xff3366, pos: { x: 0, y: 0.5, z: 0 }, size: 0.9 },
            { id: 'explorer_1', name: 'EXPLORER', type: 'explorer', status: 'idle', color: 0x00ff9d, pos: { x: 4.2, y: 1.7, z: 2.5 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'coder_1', name: 'CODER', type: 'coder', status: 'idle', color: 0xF59E0B, pos: { x: 3.8, y: 1.2, z: 1.2 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'clipper_1', name: 'CLIPPER', type: 'clipping', status: 'busy', color: 0xEC4899, pos: { x: 4.5, y: 1.8, z: -1.5 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'analyzer_1', name: 'ANALYZER', type: 'graph', status: 'idle', color: 0xEF4444, pos: { x: 3.2, y: 2.2, z: -3.0 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'architect_1', name: 'ARCHITECT', type: 'architect', status: 'busy', color: 0x3B82F6, pos: { x: -4.0, y: 2.0, z: 2.8 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'general_1', name: 'GENERAL', type: 'general', status: 'ready', color: 0x8B5CF6, pos: { x: -3.5, y: 1.5, z: 1.3 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'searcher_1', name: 'SEARCHER', type: 'search', status: 'idle', color: 0x14B8A6, pos: { x: -4.3, y: 1.9, z: -1.8 }, size: 0.6 },
            { id: 'integrator_1', name: 'INTEGRATOR', type: 'integrator', status: 'ready', color: 0xF97316, pos: { x: -3.0, y: 1.4, z: -3.2 }, size: 0.6 }
        ];
        
        // Связи
        const links = agents.filter(a => a.id !== 'batuta').map(agent => ({
            source: 'batuta',
            target: agent.id,
            type: 'orchestrates'
        }));
        
        links.push(
            { source: 'architect_1', target: 'coder_1', type: 'assigns' },
            { source: 'coder_1', target: 'general_1', type: 'consults' },
            { source: 'explorer_1', target: 'analyzer_1', type: 'feeds' },
            { source: 'clipper_1', target: 'integrator_1', type: 'sends' },
            { source: 'searcher_1', target: 'explorer_1', type: 'assists' }
        );

        // --- ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ СЦЕНЫ ---
        const scene = new THREE.Scene();
        scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x050510);
        scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x050510, 0.015);
        
        const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000);
        camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
        
        const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
        renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        renderer.setPixelRatio(window.devicePixelRatio);
        renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
        document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
        
        const labelRenderer = new CSS2DRenderer();
        labelRenderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.position = 'absolute';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.top = '0px';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.left = '0px';
        labelRenderer.domElement.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        document.body.appendChild(labelRenderer.domElement);
        
        const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
        controls.enableDamping = true;
        controls.dampingFactor = 0.05;
        controls.rotateSpeed = 1.5;
        controls.zoomSpeed = 1.2;
        controls.panSpeed = 0.8;
        controls.enableZoom = true;
        controls.enablePan = true;
        controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
        
        // --- ОСВЕЩЕНИЕ ---
        const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0x1a1a3a);
        scene.add(ambientLight);
        
        const mainLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffffff, 1);
        mainLight.position.set(5, 10, 7);
        mainLight.castShadow = true;
        scene.add(mainLight);
        
        const fillLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x4466cc, 0.4);
        fillLight.position.set(-3, 2, 4);
        scene.add(fillLight);
        
        const backLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffaa44, 0.3);
        backLight.position.set(0, 1, -5);
        scene.add(backLight);
        
        const neonLight = new THREE.PointLight(0x00ff9d, 0.5);
        neonLight.position.set(0, -1, 0);
        scene.add(neonLight);
        
        // --- ВСПОМОГАТЕЛЬНЫЕ ЭЛЕМЕНТЫ ---
        const gridHelper = new THREE.GridHelper(18, 50, 0x00ff9d, 0x2a4a6a);
        gridHelper.position.y = -1.0;
        gridHelper.material.transparent = true;
        gridHelper.material.opacity = 0.25;
        scene.add(gridHelper);
        
        const ringGeometry = new THREE.RingGeometry(2.5, 4.5, 32);
        const ringMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x00ff9d, emissive: 0x00aa55, emissiveIntensity: 0.2, side: THREE.DoubleSide, transparent: true, opacity: 0.3 });
        const ringFloor = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeometry, ringMaterial);
        ringFloor.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2;
        ringFloor.position.y = -0.99;
        scene.add(ringFloor);
        
        const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const starCount = 1200;
        const starPositions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);
        for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {
            starPositions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 300;
            starPositions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 150;
            starPositions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 100 - 50;
        }
        starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));
        const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ color: 0x88aaff, size: 0.08, transparent: true, opacity: 0.4 });
        const stars = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);
        scene.add(stars);
        
        // --- СОЗДАНИЕ КУБИКОВ И КАРТОЧЕК ---
        const agentsMap = new Map();
        const particleSystems = new Map();
        const cubes = [];
        const cards = [];
        const links3d = [];
        
        function createNeonCube(agent) {
            const group = new THREE.Group();
            const geometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(agent.size, agent.size, agent.size);
            const material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                emissive: agent.color,
                emissiveIntensity: 0.4,
                metalness: 0.8,
                roughness: 0.2,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.92
            });
            const cube = new THREE.Mesh(geometry, material);
            cube.castShadow = true;
            group.add(cube);
            
            const edgesGeo = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(geometry);
            const edgesMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: agent.color });
            const wireframe = new THREE.LineSegments(edgesGeo, edgesMat);
            group.add(wireframe);
            
            const glowGeometry = new THREE.BoxGeometry(agent.size + 0.12, agent.size + 0.12, agent.size + 0.12);
            const glowMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.15,
                side: THREE.BackSide
            });
            const glowCube = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeometry, glowMaterial);
            group.add(glowCube);
            
            group.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y, agent.pos.z);
            return group;
        }
        
        function createAgentCard(agent) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            const colorStyle = '#' + agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
            
            div.style.backgroundColor = 'rgba(8,12,25,0.92)';
            div.style.border = `1.5px solid ${colorStyle}`;
            div.style.borderRadius = '8px';
            div.style.padding = '8px 14px';
            div.style.color = '#b7c9e2';
            div.style.fontFamily = "'Share Tech Mono', monospace";
            div.style.fontSize = '11px';
            div.style.backdropFilter = 'blur(8px)';
            div.style.textAlign = 'center';
            div.style.minWidth = '130px';
            div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${colorStyle}40`;
            div.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            div.style.transition = 'all 0.2s';
            
            const statusColor = agent.status === 'idle' ? '#00ff9d' : (agent.status === 'busy' ? '#ff4444' : '#ffaa44');
            const statusText = agent.status === 'idle' ? 'IDLE' : (agent.status === 'busy' ? 'BUSY' : 'READY');
            
            div.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold; color: ${colorStyle}; margin-bottom: 4px;">${agent.name}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 9px; opacity: 0.7;">${agent.type}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 10px; margin-top: 5px;"><span style="color: ${statusColor}">${statusText}</span></div>
            `;
            
            div.onmouseenter = () => {
                div.style.transform = 'scale(1.05)';
                div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 25px ${colorStyle}`;
                showTooltip(agent);
            };
            div.onmouseleave = () => {
                div.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
                div.style.boxShadow = `0 0 15px ${colorStyle}40`;
                hideTooltip();
            };
            
            const label = new CSS2DObject(div);
            label.position.set(agent.pos.x, agent.pos.y + 1.0, agent.pos.z);
            return label;
        }
        
        function createParticleSystem(agent) {
            const particleCount = agent.id === 'batuta' ? 28 : 12;
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
            const positions = new Float32Array(particleCount * 3);
            
            for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
                const radius = agent.size + 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.7;
                const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                positions[i*3] = agent.pos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;
                positions[i*3+1] = agent.pos.y - 0.4 + Math.random() * 1.8;
                positions[i*3+2] = agent.pos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;
            }
            
            geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
            
            const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({
                color: agent.color,
                size: 0.05,
                transparent: true,
                opacity: 0.5,
                blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
            });
            
            const particleSystem = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
            
            particleSystem.userData = {
                velocities: Array(particleCount).fill().map(() => 0.006 + Math.random() * 0.01),
                agentPos: { x: agent.pos.x, y: agent.pos.y, z: agent.pos.z },
                agentSize: agent.size,
                particleCount: particleCount
            };
            
            return particleSystem;
        }
        
        function updateParticles(deltaTime) {
            const speedMultiplier = document.getElementById('upwardSpeed') ? parseFloat(document.getElementById('upwardSpeed').value) : 0.008;
            particleSystems.forEach((particles) => {
                const data = particles.userData;
                const positions = particles.geometry.attributes.position.array;
                const speedFactor = deltaTime * 60 * (speedMultiplier / 0.008);
                
                for (let i = 0; i < data.particleCount; i++) {
                    let y = positions[i*3+1];
                    y += data.velocities[i] * speedFactor;
                    
                    if (y > data.agentPos.y + 1.3) {
                        y = data.agentPos.y - 0.5;
                        const radius = data.agentSize + 0.4 + Math.random() * 0.6;
                        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                        positions[i*3] = data.agentPos.x + Math.cos(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                        positions[i*3+2] = data.agentPos.z + Math.sin(angle) * radius + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
                    }
                    
                    positions[i*3+1] = y;
                }
                
                particles.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate = true;
            });
        }
        
        function createCurvedLink(source, target, type) {
            const start = new THREE.Vector3(source.pos.x, source.pos.y + 0.2, source.pos.z);
            const end = new THREE.Vector3(target.pos.x, target.pos.y + 0.2, target.pos.z);
            
            const mid = new THREE.Vector3().addVectors(start, end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
            const direction = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(end, start).normalize();
            const perpendicular = new THREE.Vector3(-direction.z, Math.abs(direction.y) + 0.5, direction.x).normalize();
            
            mid.x += perpendicular.x * 1.2;
            mid.y += 1.0;
            mid.z += perpendicular.z * 1.2;
            
            const curve = new THREE.QuadraticBezierCurve3(start, mid, end);
            const points = curve.getPoints(50);
            const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(points);
            
            const linkColor = type === 'orchestrates' ? 0x00ff9d : (type === 'assigns' ? 0x3B82F6 : 0xF59E0B);
            const material = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: linkColor, transparent: true, opacity: 0.5 });
            const line = new THREE.Line(geometry, material);
            
            const flowParticles = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
                const particleGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.04, 8, 8);
                const particleMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: linkColor, emissive: linkColor, emissiveIntensity: 0.5 });
                const particle = new THREE.Mesh(particleGeo, particleMat);
                particle.userData = { progress: i / 3, speed: 0.0008 + Math.random() * 0.0004 };
                scene.add(particle);
                flowParticles.push(particle);
            }
            
            return { line, curve, flowParticles };
        }
        
        function updateLinkParticles() {
            const speedMultiplier = document.getElementById('particleSpeed') ? parseFloat(document.getElementById('particleSpeed').value) : 0.0008;
            links3d.forEach(link => {
                if (!link.flowParticles) return;
                link.flowParticles.forEach((particle, idx) => {
                    particle.userData.progress += particle.userData.speed * (speedMultiplier / 0.0008);
                    if (particle.userData.progress >= 1) particle.userData.progress = 0;
                    if (link.curve) {
                        const point = link.curve.getPoint(particle.userData.progress);
                        particle.position.copy(point);
                    }
                    const scale = 0.8 + Math.sin(Date.now() * 0.002 + idx) * 0.15;
                    particle.scale.set(scale, scale, scale);
                });
            });
        }
        
        const tooltip = document.getElementById('tooltip');
        
        function showTooltip(agent) {
            const colorStyle = '#' + agent.color.toString(16).padStart(6, '0');
            tooltip.style.display = 'block';
            tooltip.style.borderColor = colorStyle;
            tooltip.innerHTML = `
                <strong style="color: ${colorStyle}">${agent.name}</strong><br>
                Тип: ${agent.type}<br>
                Статус: ${agent.status}<br>
                Скилы: ${agent.id === 'coder_1' ? 'Python, JS, FastAPI' : 
                          agent.id === 'architect_1' ? 'Design, UML, Planning' :
                          agent.id === 'explorer_1' ? 'Discovery, Research' : 'Various'}
            `;
        }
        
        function hideTooltip() { tooltip.style.display = 'none'; }
        function updateTooltipPosition(event) {
            tooltip.style.left = (event.clientX + 15) + 'px';
            tooltip.style.top = (event.clientY - 10) + 'px';
        }
        
        // Построение сцены
        agents.forEach(agent => {
            const cube = createNeonCube(agent);
            scene.add(cube);
            cubes.push({ cube, agent });
            
            const card = createAgentCard(agent);
            scene.add(card);
            cards.push(card);
            
            const particles = createParticleSystem(agent);
            scene.add(particles);
            particleSystems.set(agent.id, particles);
            agentsMap.set(agent.id, agent);
        });
        
        const agentPositions = new Map();
        agents.forEach(a => agentPositions.set(a.id, a));
        
        links.forEach(link => {
            const source = agentPositions.get(link.source);
            const target = agentPositions.get(link.target);
            if (source && target) {
                const curvedLink = createCurvedLink(source, target, link.type);
                scene.add(curvedLink.line);
                links3d.push(curvedLink);
            }
        });
        
        // --- UI ОБРАБОТЧИКИ ---
        // Сворачивание панели
        const panelHeader = document.getElementById('panelHeader');
        const panelContent = document.getElementById('panelContent');
        const panelToggleIcon = document.getElementById('panelToggleIcon');
        let panelCollapsed = false;
        
        if (panelHeader) {
            panelHeader.addEventListener('click', () => {
                panelCollapsed = !panelCollapsed;
                if (panelCollapsed) {
                    panelContent.style.maxHeight = '0';
                    panelContent.style.padding = '0 15px';
                    panelToggleIcon.style.transform = 'rotate(-90deg)';
                } else {
                    panelContent.style.maxHeight = '70vh';
                    panelContent.style.padding = '12px 15px';
                    panelToggleIcon.style.transform = 'rotate(0deg)';
                }
            });
        }
        
        // Чекбоксы
        document.getElementById('toggleParticles').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            particleSystems.forEach(ps => { ps.visible = e.target.checked; });
        });
        document.getElementById('toggleStars').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            stars.visible = e.target.checked;
        });
        document.getElementById('toggleGrid').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            gridHelper.visible = e.target.checked;
            ringFloor.visible = e.target.checked;
        });
        document.getElementById('toggleLinks').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
            links3d.forEach(link => { link.line.visible = e.target.checked; });
        });
        
        // Яркость связей
        document.getElementById('edgeBrightness').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const val = parseFloat(e.target.value);
            links3d.forEach(link => {
                link.line.material.opacity = val;
            });
        });
        
        // Кнопки
        document.getElementById('viewTopBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            camera.position.set(0, 20, 0);
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            controls.update();
        });
        
        document.getElementById('resetCameraBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            camera.position.set(8, 6, 10);
            controls.target.set(0, 0, 0);
            controls.update();
        });
        
        document.getElementById('resetFilters').addEventListener('click', () => {
            const filters = ['filterExplorer', 'filterArchitect', 'filterCoder', 'filterGeneral', 'filterClipping', 'filterSearch', 'filterGraph', 'filterIntegrator'];
            filters.forEach(id => {
                const cb = document.getElementById(id);
                if (cb) cb.checked = true;
            });
        });
        
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', updateTooltipPosition);
        
        // --- АНИМАЦИЯ ---
        let time = 0;
        let frameCount = 0;
        let lastFpsUpdate = performance.now();
        let lastFrameTime = performance.now();
        let particlesVisible = true;
        
        function animateCubes(deltaTime) {
            time += deltaTime;
            cubes.forEach(({ cube, agent }) => {
                const intensity = 0.3 + Math.sin(time * 2) * 0.15;
                cube.children[0].material.emissiveIntensity = agent.status === 'busy' ? intensity + 0.3 : intensity;
                cube.children[2].material.opacity = 0.12 + Math.sin(time * 3) * 0.05;
            });
            neonLight.intensity = 0.4 + Math.sin(time * 1.5) * 0.15;
        }
        
        function animate() {
            const now = performance.now();
            let delta = Math.min(0.033, (now - lastFrameTime) / 1000);
            lastFrameTime = now;
            
            // FPS счётчик
            frameCount++;
            if (now - lastFpsUpdate >= 1000) {
                const fpsElement = document.getElementById('fpsValue');
                if (fpsElement) fpsElement.textContent = frameCount;
                frameCount = 0;
                lastFpsUpdate = now;
            }
            
            updateParticles(delta);
            updateLinkParticles();
            animateCubes(delta);
            
            stars.rotation.y += 0.0005;
            stars.rotation.x += 0.0003;
            ringFloor.rotation.z += 0.002;
            
            controls.update();
            renderer.render(scene, camera);
            labelRenderer.render(scene, camera);
            
            requestAnimationFrame(animate);
        }
        
        animate();
        console.log('✅ 3D граф агентов загружен!', agents.length, 'агентов,', links.length, 'связей');
    </script>
</body>
</html>

2. Вращение давай сделаем не пендулюм, а из операторских движений что-то - пролет по овальной орбите с фокусом граф или может что-то интересней (плавность хода камеры как сейчас норм)

# 🏛️ ХРАНИТЕЛЬ АРХИТЕКТУРЫ: ПОЛНАЯ ВЕРСИЯ С ОРБИТАЛЬНЫМ ПРОЛЕТОМ

```bash
            transition: all 0.3s;
        }
        
        button:hover {
            background: #00ff9d;

        }
        
        .orbit-toggle {
            background: #1a2a3a;

        .agent-tooltip {
            position: absolute;

        input[type="range"] {

        input[type="range"]:focus {

        input[type="range"]::-webkit-slider-thumb {

        }
```
